  const IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY = 'meganav-featureflags-ts';
  const IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
  function returnIXPDataFromCacheIfValid() {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
      return null;
    }

    try {
      const rawFlags = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);
      if (!rawFlags || rawFlags === '{}') {
        return null;
      }

      const parsedFlags = JSON.parse(rawFlags);
      if (!parsedFlags || typeof parsedFlags !== 'object') {
        return null;
      }

      const rawTimestamp = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);
      const now = Date.now();

      if (!rawTimestamp) {
        return null;
      }

      const cachedAt = Number(rawTimestamp);
      if (Number.isFinite(cachedAt) && now - cachedAt < IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
        return parsedFlags;
      }

      sessionStorage.removeItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);
      sessionStorage.removeItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);
      return null;
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Failed to read IXP cache from sessionStorage:', error);
      return null;
    }
  }
   // adding this dummy function here to avoid page break of buy/internet page. that page is calling this function
  function unmountGlobalNavScripts() {
    console.log('unmountGlobalNavScripts called latest..');
    const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';
    if (isNativeApp) {
      const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');
      gnavHeaderId?.remove();
    }
  }
  function getMeganavCookie(lookup) {
    let nameEQ = 'accessDomain' + '=';
    const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
    if (typeof lookup !== 'undefined') {
      nameEQ = lookup + '=';
    }
    for (let c of ca) {
      while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') {
        c = c.substring(1, c.length);
      }
      if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) {
        return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
      }
    }
  }
  const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';
  if (isNativeApp) {
    const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');
    gnavHeaderId?.remove();

    // csrInitialization.fetchCMSDataCSR reads this Promise from window instead of issuing a new call.

And when you travel abroad in the future, International Day Pass is already on your device so you&#39;re all set for your next international trip.</p>
</div></div><div></div></div></div><div class="border-bottom border-gray-300"><h3><button id="faq-1-btn" aria-controls="faq-1" aria-expanded="false" class="accordion-cta btn-reset heading- line-h-normal letter-spacing-0 type-lg width-full flex flex-items-top justify-between text-left pad-t-md pad-b-md" style="cursor:pointer"><span class="reading-width">Which destinations are included in International Day Pass?</span><i class="icon-chevron animate mar-l-sm-all font-bold type-lg rotate90-after"></i></button></h3><div aria-hidden="true" id="faq-1" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-1-btn" style="max-height:0px" data-comp="accordion-item-duc" class="jsx-3015755231 overflow-hidden animate-slide-close hide-focusable"><div><div class="type-base rte-styles"><p>International Day Pass works in over 210 destinations around the world and on more than 400 cruise ships. You can use it in places like Asia, Australia, Europe, South America and more.</p>

<p><br />
<a href="/support/article/wireless/KM1395068/" target="_self">Learn more about which airlines and international flights are included with AT&amp;T International Day Pass</a></p>

<p><br />
Keep in mind that airlines and flights can change at any time without warning. Even if you&#39;re flying with a listed airline, your flight might not be included.</p>

<p><br />
<a href="/support/article/wireless/KM1395068/?tabIndex=2" target="_self">Learn more about which cruise ships are included with AT&amp;T International Day Pass</a></p>
</div></div><div></div></div></div><div class="border-bottom border-gray-300"><h3><button id="faq-2-btn" aria-controls="faq-2" aria-expanded="false" class="accordion-cta btn-reset heading- line-h-normal letter-spacing-0 type-lg width-full flex flex-items-top justify-between text-left pad-t-md pad-b-md" style="cursor:pointer"><span class="reading-width">When am I charged for International Day Pass?</span><i class="icon-chevron animate mar-l-sm-all font-bold type-lg rotate90-after"></i></button></h3><div aria-hidden="true" id="faq-2" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-2-btn" style="max-height:0px" data-comp="accordion-item-duc" class="jsx-3015755231 overflow-hidden animate-slide-close hide-focusable"><div><div class="type-base rte-styles"><p>The first time you use data, send a text, or make or accept a phone call in an included destination or on an included ship or airline, your 24-hour International Day Pass begins and you will be charged a daily fee. You won&#39;t trigger another daily fee until the next time you use your device &nbsp;after that 24-hour period ends. The daily fees are calculated and charged to your bill at the end of your billing cycle.</p>
</div></div><div></div></div></div><div class="border-bottom border-gray-300"><h3><button id="faq-3-btn" aria-controls="faq-3" aria-expanded="false" class="accordion-cta btn-reset heading- line-h-normal letter-spacing-0 type-lg width-full flex flex-items-top justify-between text-left pad-t-md pad-b-md" style="cursor:pointer"><span class="reading-width">What destinations can I call with International Day Pass while traveling abroad?</span><i class="icon-chevron animate mar-l-sm-all font-bold type-lg rotate90-after"></i></button></h3><div aria-hidden="true" id="faq-3" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-3-btn" style="max-height:0px" data-comp="accordion-item-duc" class="jsx-3015755231 overflow-hidden animate-slide-close hide-focusable"><div><div class="type-base rte-styles"><p>If you&rsquo;re in an International Day Pass destination, you can call the destination&nbsp;you&rsquo;re in, any other International Day Pass destination and back to the U.S. for no additional charge. Calls to destinations not included in International Day Pass will be billed at International Long Distance rates. Per-minute pay-per-use rates apply unless an International Long Distance package is added to the device placing the call. Go to&nbsp;<a href="/international/long-distance/" rel="noreferrer noopener">att.com/internationalcalling</a>&nbsp;to review rates and options.</p>
</div></div><div></div></div></div><div class="border-bottom border-gray-300"><h3><button id="faq-4-btn" aria-controls="faq-4" aria-expanded="false" class="accordion-cta btn-reset heading- line-h-normal letter-spacing-0 type-lg width-full flex flex-items-top justify-between text-left pad-t-md pad-b-md" style="cursor:pointer"><span class="reading-width">What is International Day Pass?</span><i class="icon-chevron animate mar-l-sm-all font-bold type-lg rotate90-after"></i></button></h3><div aria-hidden="true" id="faq-4" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-4-btn" style="max-height:0px" data-comp="accordion-item-duc" class="jsx-3015755231 overflow-hidden animate-slide-close hide-focusable"><div><div class="type-base rte-styles"><p>AT&amp;T customers with an unlimited plan can get a great value abroad with International Day Pass. AT&amp;T International Day Pass lets you use your phone as you do at home for $12 per day on land. And, you can add additional lines for only $6 per day. This includes unlimited data,* talk, and text with your eligible AT&amp;T unlimited plan. Plus, <a href="/support/article/wireless/KM1395068" target="_self">International Day Pass covers more than 210 destinations</a>.</p>

<p>If you&rsquo;re taking a cruise abroad and plan to use your cell phone on land and sea or at sea only, you can stay connected for $20 per day (available on 400+ cruise ships). This includes unlimited* talk, text and data in port and on excursions.</p>

<p>*When added to your unlimited plan. Coverage and data speed vary by destination and may be changed.&nbsp;</p>
</div></div><div></div></div></div><div class="border-bottom border-gray-300"><h3><button id="faq-5-btn" aria-controls="faq-5" aria-expanded="false" class="accordion-cta btn-reset heading- line-h-normal letter-spacing-0 type-lg width-full flex flex-items-top justify-between text-left pad-t-md pad-b-md" style="cursor:pointer"><span class="reading-width">How will I be charged if I travel to different places that are part of the International Day Pass?</span><i class="icon-chevron animate mar-l-sm-all font-bold type-lg rotate90-after"></i></button></h3><div aria-hidden="true" id="faq-5" role="region" aria-labelledby="faq-5-btn" style="max-height:0px" data-comp="accordion-item-duc" class="jsx-3015755231 overflow-hidden animate-slide-close hide-focusable"><div><div class="type-base rte-styles"><p>If you&rsquo;re traveling on land only, you will only be charged one daily fee per 24-hour period for International Day Pass, even if you travel to multiple included destinations in the same 24-hour period.</p>

<p>If you&rsquo;re traveling at sea only, you will be charged one daily fee per 24-hour period for International Day Pass.</p>

<p>If you&rsquo;re traveling at sea and on land on the same day, you&rsquo;ll be charged a fee of $20 per day that includes both locations.</p>
